  <script>
    // JavaScript Document 
    /*==导航条下拉==*/
    $(function () {
      //鼠标移开隐藏
      $("div.navmenu").mouseleave(function () {
        $("ul.navtit li.on").removeClass("on");
        $(".navmenu div").hide();
      });
      //鼠标移上显示
      var mk = Number(0);
      $("ul.navtit li").eq(mk).addClass("on");
      $(".navmenu div").eq(mk).show();
      $("ul.navtit li").each(function (obj) {
        $(this).mouseenter(function () {
          $(".navmenu div").each(function () {
            $(this).hide();
          });
          $("ul.navtit li.on").removeClass("on");
          $(this).addClass("on");
          $(".navmenu div").eq(obj).show();
        });
      });
    });
    //通知公告滚动
    function AutoScroll(obj) {
      $(obj)
        .find("ul:first")
        .animate(
          {
            marginTop: "-20px",
          },
          500,
          function () {
            $(this).css({ marginTop: "0px" }).find("li:first").appendTo(this);
          }
        );
    }
    $(document).ready(function () {
      setInterval('AutoScroll("#scrollDiv")', 3000);
    });

    /*==国资杂志自动切换==*/
    $(document).ready(function () {
      $(".notes-btn a:first").addClass("on");
      $(".notes-pic:first").css("display", "block");
      autoroll();
      hookThumb();
    });
    var i = -1; //第i+1个tab开始
    var offset = 4000; //轮换时间
    var timer = null;
    function autoroll() {
      n = $(".notes-btn a").length - 1;
      i++;
      if (i > n) {
        i = 0;
      }
      slide(i);
      timer = window.setTimeout(autoroll, offset);
    }
    function slide(i) {
      $(".notes-btn a").eq(i).addClass("on").siblings().removeClass("on");
      $(".notes-pic")
        .eq(i)
        .css("display", "block")
        .siblings(".notes-pic")
        .css("display", "none");
    }
    function hookThumb() {
      $(".notes-btn a").hover(
        function () {
          if (timer) {
            clearTimeout(timer);
            i = $(this).prevAll().length;
            slide(i);
          }
        },
        function () {
          timer = window.setTimeout(autoroll, offset);
          this.blur();
          return false;
        }
      );
    }
    /*=公共服务平台tab切换=*/
    $(function () {
      var mk = Number(0);
      $("ul.fwpttit li span").eq(mk).addClass("on");
      $("div.fwptcon ul").eq(mk).show();
      $("ul.fwpttit li span").each(function (obj) {
        $(this).hover(function () {
          $("div.fwptcon ul").each(function () {
            $(this).hide();
          });
          $("ul.fwpttit li span.on").removeClass("on");
          $(this).addClass("on");
          $("div.fwptcon ul").eq(obj).show();
        });
      });
    });

    $(function () {
      (function fitMobile() {
        var isweb = "1";
        var ua = navigator.userAgent;
        if (
          /AppleWebKit.*Mobile/i.test(ua) ||
          /Android/i.test(ua) ||
          /BlackBerry/i.test(ua) ||
          /IEMobile/i.test(ua) ||
          /MIDP|SymbianOS|NOKIA|SAMSUNG|LG|NEC|TCL|Alcatel|BIRD|DBTEL|Dopod|PHILIPS|HAIER|LENOVO|MOT-|Nokia|SonyErion|SIE-|Amoi|ZTE/.test(
            ua
          )
        ) {
          isweb = "0";
          $("html").addClass("mobile");
        } else {
          isweb = "1";
        }
        function fontSize() {
          var deviceWidth = $(window).width();
          if (deviceWidth > 768) {
            deviceWidth = 768;
          }
          var fontSize = deviceWidth / 7.68;
          $("html").css("fontSize", fontSize);
        }
        fontSize();
        $(window).resize(function () {
          fontSize();
        });
      })();

      // $(".navmenu div").eq(5).find("a:last-child").attr("href", "#nav_service_plat");

      $.fn.tab = function (option) {
        var defaults = {
          oEvent: "hover",
          auto: true,
          index: 0,
        };
        var option = $.extend(defaults, option);
        var $this = $(this);

        $this.find("div.qhtab ul:first").show();

        if (option.oEvent == "hover") {
          $this.find("ul.tabtit li").hover(function () {
            var $li = $(this);

            option.index = $li.index();

            $li.addClass("on").siblings().removeClass();

            $li
              .parent()
              .siblings(".qhtab")
              .find("ul")
              .eq(option.index)
              .show()
              .siblings("ul")
              .hide();
          });
        }
      };

      /*关闭标签函数*/
      window.hidebanner_turnRed = function () {
        $("body").removeAttr("class");
        $("#topImg").hide({ duration: 200 });
      };

      $(".xxgk-zl tbody tr:last-child td").attr("colspan", 2);

      var $aList = $(".xxgk-zl a");
      $aList.eq(0).attr("href", $aList.eq(0).attr("href") + "#nav0");
      $aList.eq(1).attr("href", $aList.eq(1).attr("href") + "#nav1");
    });

  </script>

                    $('#shenla dt').click(function () {
                      $('#shenla dt').each(function (i, items) {
                        $('#shenla dd:eq(' + i + ')').hide(100);
                        $('#shenla dt:eq(' + i + ')').attr('style', 'line-height:30px; font-size:16px; background:url(/jxsgzw/static/image/1376779.gif) right no-repeat');
                        $('#shenla dt:eq(' + i + ')').attr('class', 'bbtgray');
                      });
                      var number = $('#shenla dt').index(this);
                      $('#shenla dd:eq(' + number + ')').show(100);
                      $('#shenla dt:eq(' + number + ')').attr('style', 'background:none; line-height:30px; font-size:16px; ');
                    });

  <script>
    function clickAction(str) {
      window.open(aesDecrypt(str, "abc"));
    }

    function aesDecrypt(hexStr, aesKey) {
      var words = CryptoJS.enc.Hex.parse(hexStr);
      var ciphertext = CryptoJS.enc.Base64.stringify(words);
      var bytes = CryptoJS.AES.decrypt(ciphertext, aesKey, {
        mode: CryptoJS.mode.ECB,
        padding: CryptoJS.pad.ZeroPadding
      });
      var originalText = bytes.toString(CryptoJS.enc.Utf8);
      return originalText;
    }
  </script>


<script>
    // 记录埋点的请求
    function buryPoint(params) {
        var buryPointUrl = '/saveAccessLog' // 埋点请求地址
        //需要引入config.js
        if(typeof  defaultConfig != 'undefined'){
            if(defaultConfig.userIdObj.siteId){
                var siteId = defaultConfig.userIdObj.siteId;
            }
            if(defaultConfig.userIdObj.deptId){
                var deptId = defaultConfig.userIdObj.deptId;
            }
            if(defaultConfig.userIdObj.tenantId){
                var tenantId = defaultConfig.userIdObj.tenantId;
            }
        }
        var siteNameMeta = $("meta[name='SiteName']");
        var siteName = siteNameMeta ? siteNameMeta.attr('content') : "江西省国有资产监督管理委员会";
        var postData = {
            siteId: '1754044774829461504' || siteId || "1754044774829461504",
            deptId: '' || deptId || "1754041065568247808",
            tenantId: '1749973237782081536' || tenantId || "1749973237782081536",
            siteName: siteName || "",
            channelId: params && params.channelId || "",
            channelName: params && params.channelName || "",
            title: params && params.title || "",
            articleId: params && params.articleId || "",
        }
        console.log('buryPoint-postData', postData);
        $.ajax({
            url: buryPointUrl,
            type: 'post',
            data: postData,
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function () {
            },
            error: function () {
            }
        })
    }

    //页面访问量
    // 首页
    if (window.websiteId) {
        if (!window.channelId && !window.manuscriptId) {
            buryPoint();
        }
    }
    // 概览页
    if (window.channelId && !window.manuscriptId) {
        var columnNameMeta = $("meta[name='ColumnName']");
        var channelName = columnNameMeta ? columnNameMeta.attr('content') : '国资委';
        buryPoint({
            channelId: channelId || "",
            channelName: channelName || "",
        });
    }
    // 细览页
    if (window.manuscriptId) {
        var columnNameMeta = $("meta[name='ColumnName']");
        var channelName = columnNameMeta ? columnNameMeta.attr('content') : '国资委';
        var titleMeta = $("meta[name='ArticleTitle']");
        var title = titleMeta ? titleMeta.attr('content') : '';
        var pubDateMeta = $("meta[name='PubDate']");
        var pubDate = pubDateMeta ? pubDateMeta.attr('content') : 'Tue Sep 22 15:20:29 CST 2026';
        buryPoint({
            channelId: channelId || "",
            channelName: channelName || "",
            title: title || "",
            articleId: manuscriptId || "",
            pubDate: pubDate || "",
            url: window.location.href || "",
        });
    }
</script>
